ポインター押下（描画開始）\n  const handlePointerDown = (e: PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    const ctx = getContext();\n    if (!canvas || !ctx) return;\n\n    // historyStackRef.current[historyStackRef.current.length - 1];\n      if (previousState) {\n        ctx.putImageData(previousState, 0, 0);\n      }\n === 0) return;\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    if (!canvas) return;\n\n    try {\n      const dataUrl = canvas.toDataURL('image/png');\n      const link = document.createElement('a');\n getCoordinates = (e: PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    if (!canvas) return { x: 0, y: 0 };\n    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();\n    const scaleX = canvas.width / rect.width;\n    const scaleY = canvas.height / rect.height;\n    return {\n      x: (e.clientX - rect.left) * scaleX,\n      y: (e.clientY - rect.top) * scaleY,\n    };\n  };\n\n  // ポインター押下（描画開始）\n  const handlePointerDown = (e: PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    const ctx = getContext();\n    if (!canvas || !ctx) ctx.lineWidth = settings.lineWidth;\n\n    if (settings.tool === 'eraser') {\n      ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';\n      ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';\n    } else {\n      ctx.strokeStyle = settings.color;\n      ctx.fillStyle = settings.color;\n    }\n\n    ctx.arc(coords.x, coords.y, settings.lineWidth / 2, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n    ctx.fill();\n    ctx.restore();\n  };\n\n  // ポインター移動（線を描く）\n  const handlePointerMove = (e: PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {\n    if (!isDrawingRef.current || !lastPointRef.current) return;\n    const ctx = getContext();\n    if (!ctx) return;\n\n    const coords = getCoordinates(e);\n\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.lineCap = 'round';\n    ctx.lineJoin = 'round';\n    ctx.lineWidth = settings.lineWidth;\n\n    if (settings.tool === 'eraser') {\n      ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';\n    } else {\n      ctx.strokeStyle = settings.color;\n    }\n\n    ctx.moveTo(lastPointRef.current.x, lastPointRef.current.y);\n {\n    if (!isDrawingRef.current) return;\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    if (canvas) {\n      try {\n        canvas.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);\n      } catch {\n        // className=\"canvas-container\" ref={containerRef}>\n      <canvas\n        ref={canvasRef}\n        width={canvasDimensions.width}\n        height={canvasDimensions.height}\n        className=\"canvas-element\"\n        onPointerDown={handlePointerDown}\n        onPointerMove={handlePointerMove}\n        onPointerUp={handlePointerEnd}\n        onPointerCancel={handlePointerEnd}\n save: number;\n}\n\n/**\n * キャンバス操作トリガーを集約管理するカスタムフック\n */\nfunction useCanvasActions() {\n  const [triggers, setTriggers] = useState<CanvasTriggers>({\n    clear: 0,\n    undo: 0,\n    save: 0,\n  });\n\n  const triggerClear = useCallback(() => {\n    setTriggers((prev) => ({ ...prev, clear: prev.clear + 1 }));\n  }, []);\n\n  const triggerUndo = useCallback(() => {\n    setTriggers((prev) => ({ ...prev, undo: prev.undo + 1 }));\n  }, []);\n\n  const triggerSave = useCallback(() => {\n    setTriggers((prev) => ({ ...prev, save: prev.save + 1 }));\n  }, []);\n\n  return { triggers, */}\n      <div className=\"shortcut-info\">\n        💡 マウスや指で自由に描画できます。スマホやタブレットのタッチ操作にも対応しています。\n      </div>\n    </aside>\n '../types';\n\nexport default function CanvasBoard({\n  settings,\n  clearTrigger,\n  undoTrigger,\n  onSaveTrigger,\n  onUndoStatusChange,\n}: CanvasBoardProps) {\n  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);\n  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);\n  const isDrawingRef = useRef<boolean>(false);\n  const lastPointRef = useRef<{ x: number; y: number } | null>(null);\n\n  // オフスクリーンCanvasによる履歴管理スタック（サイズ非依存で安全）\n  const historyStackRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement[]>([]);\n  const MAX_HISTORY = 30;\n\n  // 初期化フラグと前回トリガー追跡用\n  const isInitializedRef = useRef<boolean>(false);\n  const prevClearTriggerRef = useRef<number>(clearTrigger);\n  const prevUndoTriggerRef = useRef<number>(undoTrigger);\n  const prevSaveTriggerRef = useRef<number>(onSaveTrigger);\n\n  // キャンバスコンテキスト取得（高DPIスケーリング維持）\n  const getContext = useCallback((): CanvasRenderingContext2D | null => {\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    if (!canvas) return null;\n    return canvas.getContext('2d');\n  }, []);\n\n  // 現在の状態をオフスクリーンキャンバスとして履歴に保存\n  const pushHistory = useCallback(() => {\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    if (!canvas) return;\n\n    try {\n      const backup = document.createElement('canvas');\n      backup.width = canvas.width;\n      backup.height = canvas.height;\n      const backupCtx = backup.getContext('2d');\n      if (backupCtx) {\n        backupCtx.drawImage(canvas, 0, 0);\n        historyStackRef.current.push(backup);\n        if (historyStackRef.current.length > MAX_HISTORY) {\n          historyStackRef.current.shift();\n        }\n        onUndoStatusChange?.(historyStackRef.current.length > 1);\n      }\n    } catch {\n      // 履歴保存失敗対策\n    }\n  }, [onUndoStatusChange]);\n\n  // 高DPI対応とリサイズ時の描画保持処理\n  const resizeCanvasAndPreserve = useCallback(() => {\n    const container = containerRef.current;\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    if (!container || !canvas) return;\n\n    const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();\n    const cssWidth = Math.max(300, Math.floor(rect.width));\n    const cssHeight = Math.max(300, Math.floor(rect.height));\n    const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;\n\n    const newPixelWidth = Math.floor(cssWidth * dpr);\n    const newPixelHeight = Math.floor(cssHeight * dpr);\n\n    // サイズに変更がない場合はスキップ\n    if (\n      canvas.width === newPixelWidth &&\n      canvas.height === newPixelHeight &&\n      isInitializedRef.current\n    ) {\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // 既存の描画内容を退避\n    let tempCanvas: HTMLCanvasElement | null = null;\n    if (isInitializedRef.current && canvas.width > 0 && canvas.height > 0) {\n      tempCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');\n      tempCanvas.width = canvas.width;\n      tempCanvas.height = canvas.height;\n      const tempCtx = tempCanvas.getContext('2d');\n      if (tempCtx) {\n        tempCtx.drawImage(canvas, 0, 0);\n      }\n    }\n\n    // キャンバスピクセルサイズと表示サイズの設定\n    canvas.width = newPixelWidth;\n    canvas.height = newPixelHeight;\n    canvas.style.width = `${cssWidth}px`;\n    canvas.style.height = `${cssHeight}px`;\n\n    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n    if (!ctx) return;\n\n    // 初回初期化または退避データがない場合は白背景\n    ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);\n    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';\n    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, newPixelWidth, newPixelHeight);\n\n    if (tempCanvas) {\n      // リサイズ前の描画内容を復元\n      ctx.drawImage(tempCanvas, 0, 0);\n    }\n\n    // 次回描画用にDPIスケールを適用\n    ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);\n\n    if (!isInitializedRef.current) {\n      isInitializedRef.current = true;\n      pushHistory();\n    }\n  }, [pushHistory]);\n\n  // 初期化 & ResizeObserverによるレスポンシブ追従\n  useEffect(() => {\n    resizeCanvasAndPreserve();\n\n    let resizeTimer: number;\n    const handleResize = () => {\n      window.clearTimeout(resizeTimer);\n      resizeTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {\n        resizeCanvasAndPreserve();\n      }, 100);\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n    return () => {\n      window.removeEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n      window.clearTimeout(resizeTimer);\n    };\n  }, [resizeCanvasAndPreserve]);\n\n  // 全消去トリガーの監視\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (clearTrigger === prevClearTriggerRef.current) return;\n    prevClearTriggerRef.current = clearTrigger;\n\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    const ctx = getContext();\n    if (!canvas || !ctx) return;\n\n    const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);\n    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';\n    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);\n    ctx.restore();\n\n    ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);\n    pushHistory();\n  }, [clearTrigger, getContext, pushHistory]);\n\n  // アンドゥトリガーの監視\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (undoTrigger === prevUndoTriggerRef.current) return;\n    prevUndoTriggerRef.current = undoTrigger;\n\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    const ctx = getContext();\n    if (!canvas || !ctx) return;\n\n    if (historyStackRef.current.length > 1) {\n      historyStackRef.current.pop();\n      const previousState = historyStackRef.current[historyStackRef.current.length - 1];\n      if (previousState) {\n        const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;\n        ctx.save();\n        ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);\n        ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';\n        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);\n        ctx.drawImage(previousState, 0, 0);\n        ctx.restore();\n        ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);\n      }\n      onUndoStatusChange?.(historyStackRef.current.length > 1);\n    }\n  }, [undoTrigger, getContext, onUndoStatusChange]);\n\n  // 画像保存（PNGダウンロード）トリガーの監視\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (onSaveTrigger === prevSaveTriggerRef.current) return;\n    prevSaveTriggerRef.current = onSaveTrigger;\n\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    if (!canvas) return;\n\n    try {\n      const dataUrl = canvas.toDataURL('image/png');\n      const link = document.createElement('a');\n      const timestamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, '-').slice(0, 19);\n      link.download = `drawing-${timestamp}.png`;\n      link.href = dataUrl;\n      document.body.appendChild(link);\n      link.click();\n      document.body.removeChild(link);\n    } catch {\n      // エラー時のフォールバック\n    }\n  }, [onSaveTrigger]);\n\n  // ポインター座標の計算（CSSピクセル基準）\n  const getCoordinates = (e: PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    if (!canvas) return { x: 0, y: 0 };\n    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();\n    return {\n      x: e.clientX - rect.left,\n      y: e.clientY - rect.top,\n    };\n  };\n\n  // ポインター押下（描画開始）\n  const handlePointerDown = (e: PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    const ctx = getContext();\n    if (!canvas || !ctx) return;\n\n    try {\n      canvas.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);\n    } catch {\n      // キャプチャ失敗対策\n    }\n\n    isDrawingRef.current = true;\n    const coords = getCoordinates(e);\n    lastPointRef.current = coords;\n\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.lineCap = 'round';\n    ctx.lineJoin = 'round';\n    ctx.lineWidth = settings.lineWidth;\n\n    if (settings.tool === 'eraser') {\n      ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';\n      ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';\n    } else {\n      ctx.strokeStyle = settings.color;\n      ctx.fillStyle = settings.color;\n    }\n\n    ctx.arc(coords.x, coords.y, settings.lineWidth / 2, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n    ctx.fill();\n    ctx.restore();\n  };\n\n  // ポインター移動（線を描画）\n  const handlePointerMove = (e: PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {\n    if (!isDrawingRef.current || !lastPointRef.current) return;\n    const ctx = getContext();\n    if (!ctx) return;\n\n    const coords = getCoordinates(e);\n\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.lineCap = 'round';\n    ctx.lineJoin = 'round';\n    ctx.lineWidth = settings.lineWidth;\n\n    if (settings.tool === 'eraser') {\n      ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';\n    } else {\n      ctx.strokeStyle = settings.color;\n    }\n\n    ctx.moveTo(lastPointRef.current.x, lastPointRef.current.y);\n    ctx.lineTo(coords.x, coords.y);\n    ctx.stroke();\n    ctx.restore();\n\n    lastPointRef.current = coords;\n  };\n\n  // 描画終了処理\n  const handlePointerEnd = (e: PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {\n    if (!isDrawingRef.current) return;\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    if (canvas) {\n      try {\n
